## 任务：LoRA Rank Sensitivity Ablation（A1 实验）

服务器：gpu-6000pro-2 (root)
项目路径：/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/
GPU：cuda:0

### 目标
验证 depth signal 对 LoRA rank 不敏感。在 Pythia-1.4B nucleus p=0.95 cell 上，跑 rank ∈ {4, 16, 64}。

### 实验流程（每个 rank）
1. LoRA finetune Pythia-1.4B：d0→d1→d2→d3（4 轮递归，每轮 LoRA rank=r）
2. 生成文本（nucleus p=0.95，500 samples/depth）
3. 特征提取（用 Pythia-1.4B 作 reference model 计算 surprisal features）
4. RF 4-class evaluation（5-fold CV）

### 输出要求
产出 JSON 结果文件 `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/lora_rank_ablation/summary.json`：
```json
{
  "rank_4": {"4class_acc": X, "ci_95": [lo, hi], "d1v2_auc": X, "top_features": [...]},
  "rank_16": {"4class_acc": X, "ci_95": [lo, hi], "d1v2_auc": X, "top_features": [...]},
  "rank_64": {"4class_acc": X, "ci_95": [lo, hi], "d1v2_auc": X, "top_features": [...]}
}
```

### 实现方案
1. 先看现有脚本了解 pipeline 架构：
   - `scripts/run_pipeline.sh` 或类似脚本
   - `scripts/` 下的 LoRA training / generation / feature extraction / eval 脚本
2. 写一个 `scripts/run_lora_rank_ablation.sh`，循环 rank ∈ {4, 16, 64}，对每个 rank 跑完整 pipeline
3. 关键参数：
   - 模型：Pythia-1.4B（检查服务器上模型路径）
   - 生成策略：nucleus p=0.95
   - 数据量：500 samples/depth，d0-d3（4 class）
   - LoRA 训练：调整 rank 参数（其余如 alpha=2*rank, target_modules 保持不变）
   - seed=42
4. 用 `submit_training_job` 提交到 cuda:0

### 环境
- conda 环境：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
- 先检查已有环境：`pip list | grep -iE "torch|peft|transformers"`
- 预计运行时间：~3h（3 个 rank × ~1h 每个）

### 注意事项
- rank=16 是当前 benchmark 配置，结果应与已有实验一致（可作 sanity check）
- 文件头部写清楚文件用途注释
- dry-run 验证脚本能启动（跑前几步确认无 import error）

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"用 LoRA 微调后的模型无条件生成下一代文本。\\n\\nseed_id 为独立递增编号（0..num_samples-1），不与上一代数据对应。\\nmax_new_tokens 默认 512，与 depth-0 的 token 范围（256-512）一致，\\n避免生成文本系统性偏短导致分类器学到长度 confound。\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\nfrom peft import PeftModel\\n\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Generate next-depth text with LoRA model\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--adapter_dir\\\", required=True, help=\\\"LoRA adapter path\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output_path\\\", required=True, help=\\\"Output jsonl path\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--model_name\\\", default=\\\"EleutherAI/pythia-1.4b\\\", help=\\\"Base model name\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--model_cache\\\", default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\\\", help=\\\"Model cache dir\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--target_depth\\\", type=int, required=True, help=\\\"Target depth (1, 2, 3)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--num_samples\\\", type=int, default=5000, help=\\\"Number of samples to generate\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--max_new_tokens\\\", type=int, default=512)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--temperature\\\", type=float, default=1.0)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--top_p\\\", type=float, default=0.95)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--top_k\\\", type=int, default=50)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--decoding_strategy\\\", default=\\\"nucleus\\\",\\n                   choices=[\\\"nucleus\\\", \\\"topk\\\", \\\"temp_only\\\"],\\n                   help=\\\"Decoding strategy: nucleus (top_p), topk (top_k), temp_only\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--batch_size\\\", type=int, default=16)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--gpu\\\", type=int, default=0)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    args = parse_args()\\n\\n    if \\\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\\\" not in os.environ:\\n        os.environ[\\\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\\\"] = str(args.gpu)\\n    device = torch.device(\\\"cuda:0\\\")\\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Loading base model: {args.model_name}\\\")\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n        args.model_name,\\n        cache_dir=args.model_cache,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n    )\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n        args.model_name,\\n        cache_dir=args.model_cache,\\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n    )\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Loading LoRA adapter: {args.adapter_dir}\\\")\\n    model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, args.adapter_dir)\\n    model = model.to(device)\\n    model.eval()\\n\\n    total = args.num_samples\\n    print(f\\\"Generating {total} samples at depth {args.target_depth}\\\")\\n\\n    bos_token_id = tokenizer.bos_token_id\\n    if bos_token_id is None:\\n        bos_token_id = tokenizer.eos_token_id\\n\\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.output_path), exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    generated = 0\\n    with open(args.output_path, \\\"w\\\", encoding=\\\"utf-8\\\") as fout:\\n        for batch_start in range(0, total, args.batch_size):\\n            batch_end = min(batch_start + args.batch_size, total)\\n            cur_batch_size = batch_end - batch_start\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.35}}"}

results/classification_report.txt, confusion_matrix.png, pairwise_auc.json, feature_importance.json\\n\\nDependencies: numpy, scipy, sklearn, matplotlib\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport matplotlib\\nmatplotlib.use(\\\"Agg\\\")\\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import train_test_split\\nfrom sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\\nfrom sklearn.linear_model import LogisticRegression\\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import (accuracy_score, roc_auc_score,\\n                             confusion_matrix, classification_report)\\n\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Train depth classifiers on extracted features.\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--features_path\\\", default=\\\"data/features.jsonl\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output_dir\\\", default=\\\"results/\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--test_size\\\", type=float, default=0.2)\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\ndef load_features(path):\\n    records = []\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for i, line in enumerate(f):\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if not line:\\n                continue\\n            try:\\n                records.append(json.loads(line))\\n            except json.JSONDecodeError as e:\\n                print(f'WARNING: Skipping line {i+1} in {path}: {e}')\\n    exclude = {\\\"depth\\\", \\\"sample_id\\\"}\\n    feature_names = [k for k in records[0].keys() if k not in exclude]\\n    X = np.array([[r[k] for k in feature_names] for r in records], dtype=np.float64)\\n    y = np.array([r[\\\"depth\\\"] for r in records], dtype=np.int64)\\n    X = np.nan_to_num(X, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)\\n    return X, y, feature_names\\n\\n\\ndef pairwise_auc(y_true, y_prob, d1, d2, labels):\\n    mask = np.isin(y_true, [d1, d2])\\n    if mask.sum() < 2:\\n        return None\\n    yt = (y_true[mask] == d2).astype(int)\\n    if len(np.unique(yt)) < 2:\\n        return None\\n    d2_idx = list(labels).index(d2)\\n    yp = y_prob[mask, d2_idx]\\n    return float(roc_auc_score(yt, yp))\\n\\n\\ndef evaluate_all_pairs(y_true, y_prob, labels):\\n    pair_aucs = {}\\n    for i in range(len(labels)):\\n        for j in range(i + 1, len(labels)):\\n            d1, d2 = labels[i], labels[j]\\n            auc = pairwise_auc(y_true, y_prob, d1, d2, labels)\\n            pair_aucs[f\\\"depth{d1}_vs_depth{d2}\\\"] = round(auc, 4) if auc is not None else None\\n    return pair_aucs\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_ci(y_true, y_pred, metric_fn, n_boot=1000, seed=42, ci=0.95):\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    scores = []\\n    n = len(y_true)\\n    for _ in range(n_boot):\\n        idx = rng.choice(n, n, replace=True)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.35}}"}

--- Save outputs ---\\n    report_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \\\"classification_report.txt\\\")\\n    with open(report_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        f.write(\\\"\\\\n\\\".join(report_lines))\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nSaved report to {report_path}\\\")\\n\\n    best_name = \\\"Random Forest\\\" if acc_rf >= acc_lr else \\\"Logistic Regression\\\"\\n    best_pred = y_pred_rf if acc_rf >= acc_lr else y_pred_lr\\n    plot_confusion_matrix(y_test, best_pred, labels,\\n                          f\\\"{best_name} Confusion Matrix\\\",\\n                          os.path.join(args.output_dir, \\\"confusion_matrix.png\\\"))\\n\\n    all_pair_aucs = {\\\"logistic_regression\\\": pair_aucs_lr, \\\"random_forest\\\": pair_aucs_rf}\\n    auc_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \\\"pairwise_auc.json\\\")\\n    with open(auc_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(all_pair_aucs, f, indent=2)\\n    print(f\\\"Saved pairwise AUC to {auc_path}\\\")\\n\\n    fi_path = os.path.join(args.output_dir, \\\"feature_importance.json\\\")\\n    with open(fi_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(fi_sorted, f, indent=2)\\n    print(f\\\"Saved feature importance to {fi_path}\\\")\\n\\n    # --- MVP RESULT ---\\n    best_acc = max(acc_lr, acc_rf)\\n    auc_1v2 = None\\n    for m_aucs in [pair_aucs_lr, pair_aucs_rf]:\\n        v = m_aucs.get(\\\"depth1_vs_depth2\\\")\\n        if v is not None and (auc_1v2 is None or v > auc_1v2):\\n            auc_1v2 = v\\n\\n    pass_fail = \\\"PASS\\\" if (best_acc > 0.40 and auc_1v2 is not None and auc_1v2 > 0.6) else \\\"FAIL\\\"\\n\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n{'='*40}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"=== MVP RESULT ===\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"4-class accuracy: {best_acc*100:.1f}% (chance: {100/len(labels):.1f}%)\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"depth-1 vs depth-2 AUC: {auc_1v2}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"PASS/FAIL: {pass_fail}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"{'='*40}\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.12}}"

{
  "server": "gpu-6000pro-2",
  "command": "cat > /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/scripts/eval_lora_rank_ablation.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"5-fold CV evaluation for LoRA rank ablation. Outputs summary.json.\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport numpy as np\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\nfrom sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nfrom sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, roc_auc_score\n\n\ndef load_features(path):\n    records = []\n    with open(path) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if not line:\n                continue\n            records.append(json.loads(line))\n    exclude = {\"depth\", \"sample_id\"}\n    feature_names = [k for k in records[0].keys() if k not in exclude]\n    X = np.array([[r[k] for k in feature_names] for r in records], dtype=np.float64)\n    y = np.array([r[\"depth\"] for r in records], dtype=np.int64)\n    X = np.nan_to_num(X, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)\n    return X, y, feature_names\n\n\ndef pairwise_auc_from_proba(y_true, y_prob, d1, d2, labels):\n    mask = np.isin(y_true, [d1, d2])\n    if mask.sum() < 2:\n        return None\n    yt = (y_true[mask] == d2).astype(int)\n    if len(np.unique(yt)) < 2:\n        return None\n    d2_idx = list(labels).index(d2)\n    yp = y_prob[mask, d2_idx]\n    return float(roc_auc_score(yt, yp))\n\n\ndef evaluate_rank(features_path, seed=42):\n    X, y, feature_names = load_features(features_path)\n    labels = sorted(np.unique(y))\n\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=seed)\n    fold_accs = []\n    fold_d1v2_aucs = []\n    all_importances = np.zeros(len(feature_names))\n\n    for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(X, y):\n        X_train, X_test = X[train_idx], X[test_idx]\n        y_train, y_test = y[train_idx], y[test_idx]\n\n        scaler = StandardScaler()\n        X_train_s = scaler.fit_transform(X_train)\n        X_test_s = scaler.transform(X_test)\n\n        rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=100, random_state=seed)\n        rf.fit(X_train_s, y_train)\n\n        y_pred = rf.predict(X_test_s)\n        y_prob = rf.predict_proba(X_test_s)\n        # align proba columns to labels\n        prob_aligned = np.zeros((len(y_test), len(labels)))\n        for i, cls in enumerate(rf.classes_):\n            idx = list(labels).index(cls)\n            prob_aligned[:, idx] = y_prob[:, i]\n\n        fold_accs.append(accuracy_score(y_test, y_pred))\n        auc_1v2 = pairwise_auc_from_proba(y_test, prob_aligned, 1, 2, labels)\n        if auc_1v2 is not None:\n            fold_d1v2_aucs.append(auc_1v2)\n        all_importances += rf.feature_importances_\n\n    mean_acc = float(np.mean(fold_accs))\n    std_acc = float(np.std(fold_accs))\n    ci_lo = float(mean_acc - 1.96 * std_acc)\n    ci_hi = float(mean_acc + 1.96 * std_acc)\n    mean_d1v2 = float(np.mean(fold_d1v2_aucs)) if fold_d1v2_aucs else None\n\n    avg_imp = all_importances / 5\n    top_idx = np.argsort(-avg_imp)[:5]\n    top_features = [feature_names[i] for i in top_idx]\n\n    return {\n        \"4class_acc\": round(mean_acc, 4),\n        \"ci_95\": [round(ci_lo, 4), round(ci_hi, 4)],\n        \"d1v2_auc\": round(mean_d1v2, 4) if mean_d1v2 else None,\n        \"top_features\": top_features,\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    p.add_argument(\"--base_dir\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--ranks\", default=\"4,16,64\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    args = p.parse_args()\n\n    ranks = [int(r) for r in args.ranks.split(\",\")]\n    results = {}\n\n    for rank in ranks:\n        feat_path = os.path.join(args.base_dir, f\"results/lora_rank_ablation/rank_{rank}/features.jsonl\")\n        if not os.path.exists(feat_path):\n            print(f\"[SKIP] rank={rank}: {feat_path} not found\")\n            continue\n        print(f\"Evaluating rank={rank}...\")\n        results[f\"rank_{rank}\"] = evaluate_rank(feat_path, seed=args.seed)\n        print(f\"  acc={results[f'rank_{rank}']['4class_acc']}, d1v2_auc={results[f'rank_{rank}']['d1v2_auc']}\")\n\n    out_dir = os.path.join(args.base_dir, \"results/lora_rank_ablation\")\n    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    out_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"summary.json\")\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nSaved: {out_path}\")\n    print(json.dumps(results, indent=2))\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nPYEOF"
}

then\n    _aml_exit_handler() { local ec=$?; mkdir -p /tmp/agent-ml-exit; printf \"%s|%s\\n\" \"$ec\" \"$(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)\" > \"/tmp/agent-ml-exit/${AML_EXP_ID}.exit\"; }\n    trap _aml_exit_handler EXIT\nfi\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\ncd $PROJECT_DIR\n\nsource /etc/network_turbo 2>/dev/null || true\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\nexport HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\n\nif [ -f /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh ]; then\n    source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh\n    conda activate base\nfi\n\nMODEL_PATH=\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\"\nTARGET_MODULES=\"query_key_value\"\nNUM_SAMPLES=500\nSEED=42\nRANKS=\"4 16 64\"\n\necho \"========================================\"\necho \"LoRA Rank Ablation: ranks = $RANKS\"\necho \"Model: $MODEL_PATH\"\necho \"Samples/depth: $NUM_SAMPLES, seed: $SEED\"\necho \"========================================\"\n\nfor RANK in $RANKS; do\n    ALPHA=$((RANK * 2))\n    WORK_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/results/lora_rank_ablation/rank_${RANK}\"\n    DATA_DIR=\"$WORK_DIR/data\"\n    CKPT_DIR=\"$WORK_DIR/checkpoints\"\n\n    mkdir -p \"$DATA_DIR\" \"$CKPT_DIR\"\n\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"========================================\"\n    echo \"=== RANK=$RANK (alpha=$ALPHA) ===\"\n    echo \"========================================\"\n\n    # Use shared depth_0 seed data (subsample handled by extract_features --max_samples)\n    if [ ! -f \"$DATA_DIR/depth_0.jsonl\" ]; then\n        # Take first 500 lines from depth_0 for consistency\n        head -n $NUM_SAMPLES \"$PROJECT_DIR/data/depth_0.jsonl\" > \"$DATA_DIR/depth_0.jsonl\"\n    fi\n\n    # Recursive: finetune on depth_d -> generate depth_{d+1}\n    for DEPTH in 0 1 2; do\n        NEXT_DEPTH=$((DEPTH + 1))\n\n        echo \"--- LoRA finetune depth_$DEPTH (rank=$RANK) ---\"\n        python scripts/lora_finetune.py \\\n            --input_data \"$DATA_DIR/depth_${DEPTH}.jsonl\" \\\n            --output_dir \"$CKPT_DIR/lora_depth_${DEPTH}\" \\\n            --model_name \"$MODEL_PATH\" \\\n            --target_modules \"$TARGET_MODULES\" \\\n            --lora_rank $RANK \\\n            --lora_alpha $ALPHA \\\n            --seed $SEED \\\n            --gpu 0\n\n        echo \"--- Generate depth_$NEXT_DEPTH (rank=$RANK) ---\"\n        python scripts/generate_next_depth.py \\\n            --adapter_dir \"$CKPT_DIR/lora_depth_${DEPTH}\" \\\n            --output_path \"$DATA_DIR/depth_${NEXT_DEPTH}.jsonl\" \\\n            --target_depth $NEXT_DEPTH \\\n            --model_name \"$MODEL_PATH\" \\\n            --num_samples $NUM_SAMPLES \\\n            --decoding_strategy nucleus \\\n            --top_p 0.95 \\\n            --seed $SEED \\\n            --gpu 0\n    done\n\n    echo \"--- Extract features (rank=$RANK) ---\"\n    python scripts/extract_features.py \\\n        --data_dir \"$DATA_DIR\" \\\n        --model_path \"$MODEL_PATH\" \\\n        --output_path \"$WORK_DIR/features.jsonl\" \\\n        --max_samples $NUM_SAMPLES \\\n        --gpu 0\n\n    echo \"=== rank=$RANK pipeline complete ===\"\ndone\n\necho \"\"\necho \"========================================\"\necho \"=== Running 5-fold CV evaluation ===\"\necho \"========================================\"\npython scripts/eval_lora_rank_ablation.py --base_dir \"$PROJECT_DIR\" --ranks \"4,16,64\" --seed $SEED\n\necho \"\"\necho \"=== ALL DONE ===\"\nSHEOF\nchmod +x /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/scripts/run_lora_rank_ablation.sh"
